function mt(t, a) {
  if (t != null && t.isAdmin)
    return {
      label: "Admin",
      maxQuizzes: 1 / 0,
      maxSessionsPerQuiz: 1 / 0,
      aiEnabled: !0,
      hasStart: !1,
      hasExpiry: !1,
    };
  const d = t != null && t.plan && Ge[t.plan] ? t.plan : yc,
    m = Ge[d],
    f = a == null ? void 0 : a[d];
  if (!f) return m;
  const x = (b, N) => (typeof b == "number" ? (b === -1 ? 1 / 0 : b) : N);
  return {
    ...m,
    maxQuizzes: x(f.maxQuizzes, m.maxQuizzes),
    maxSessionsPerQuiz: x(f.maxSessionsPerQuiz, m.maxSessionsPerQuiz),
    price: typeof f.price == "number" && f.price >= 0 ? f.price : m.price,
  };
}

function bj(t) {

function Mx(t) {
  const a = [],
    d = typeof (t == null ? void 0 : t.title) == "string" ? t.title.trim() : "";
  d
    ? d.length > 200 &&
      a.push("The rubric name is too long (200 characters at most).")
    : a.push("Give the rubric a name.");
  const m = Array.isArray(t == null ? void 0 : t.criteria) ? t.criteria : [];
  (m.length === 0 && a.push("Add at least one criterion."),
    m.length > zx && a.push(`A rubric can have at most ${zx} criteria.`));
  let f = 0,
    x = !1,
    b = !0;
  return (
    m.forEach((N, y) => {
      const R = N && typeof N.name == "string" && N.name.trim(),
        S = R ? `"${N.name.trim()}"` : `Criterion ${y + 1}`;
      R || a.push(`Criterion ${y + 1} needs a name.`);
      const I = N && N.unit !== void 0 ? N.unit : "points";
      if (!mv.includes(I)) {
        (a.push(`${S} needs a unit — points or percentage.`), (b = !1));
        return;
      }
      const E = Number(N.maxPoints);
      if (!Number.isFinite(E) || E <= 0) {
        (a.push(`${S} needs a maximum greater than 0.`), (b = !1));
        return;
      }
      if (Math.abs(E * 100 - Math.round(E * 100)) > 1e-6) {
        (a.push(`${S}: use at most 2 decimal places.`), (b = !1));
        return;
      }
      if (I === "percentage" && E > 100) {
        (a.push(`${S}: a percentage can't be more than 100.`), (b = !1));
        return;
      }
      if (I === "points" && E > Dx) {
        (a.push(`${S}: points can't be more than ${Dx}.`), (b = !1));
        return;
      }
      I === "percentage" ? (f += Math.round(E * 100)) : (x = !0);
    }),
    b &&
      m.length > 0 &&
      (f > 1e4
        ? a.push(
            `Percentages add up to ${Rx(f / 100)}% — they can't be more than 100%.`,
          )
        : !x && f !== 1e4
          ? a.push(
              `Percentages must add up to exactly 100% (currently ${Rx(f / 100)}%).`,
            )
          : x &&
            f >= 1e4 &&
            a.push(
              "Percentages already use 100% — nothing is left for the criteria scored in points.",
            )),
    { ok: a.length === 0, errors: a }
  );
}
function yj(t, a = Date.now()) {
  return {
    ...t,

    criteria: ((t == null ? void 0 : t.criteria) || []).map((d) => ({
      id: d.id,

function Mh(t, a) {
  const d = Array.isArray(t == null ? void 0 : t.criteria) ? t.criteria : [],
    m = Number(a);
  if (!d.length || !Number.isFinite(m) || m <= 0 || m > pv) return [];
  const f = Math.round(m * 100),
    x = d.map(Ec),
    b = d.map((F) => {
      const te = Number(F.maxPoints);
      return Number.isFinite(te) && te > 0 ? Math.round(te * 100) : 0;
    });
  let N = 0,
    y = 0;
  if (
    (b.forEach((F, te) => {
      x[te] === "percentage" ? (N += F) : (y += F);
    }),
    N > 1e4)
  )
    return [];
  if (y === 0 && N !== 1e4) return [];
  if (y > 0 && N >= 1e4) return [];
  const R = y > 0 ? y : 1,
    S = 1e4 * R,
    I = b.map((F, te) =>
      x[te] === "percentage" ? f * F * R : f * (1e4 - N) * F,
    ),
    E = I.map((F) => Math.floor(F / S)),
    O = I.map((F) => F % S),
    Z = f - E.reduce((F, te) => F + te, 0),
    Y = O.map((F, te) => te).sort((F, te) => O[te] - O[F] || F - te);
  for (let F = 0; F < Z; F++) E[Y[F]] += 1;
  return d.map((F, te) => ({ ...F, scaledMax: E[te] / 100 }));
}

          0,

        );
      ((d[f.id] = N / 100), (m += N));
    }),

  );
}

                  }),

                  })

                            }),
                          ],
                        },
                        x.id,
                      ),
                    ),
                  }),
            ],
          }),
        }),
    ],
  });

class yt extends xi.Component {
  constructor() {
    super(...arguments);
    mx(this, "state", { failed: !1 });
  }
  static getDerivedStateFromError() {
    return { failed: !0 };
  }
  componentDidCatch(d) {
    console.error("A lazily-loaded part of the app failed to load:", d);
  }
  render() {
    if (this.state.failed) {
      const d = this.props.errorWrapper || xi.Fragment;
      return e.jsx(d, {
        children: e.jsxs("div", {
          role: "alert",
          className:
            "w-full max-w-[461px] mx-auto my-6 p-4 rounded-2xl text-center",
          style: {
            background: "rgba(231,76,110,0.10)",
            border: "1px solid rgba(231,76,110,0.5)",
          },
          children: [
            e.jsxs("div", {
              className: "text-sm mb-3",
              style: { lineHeight: 1.6 },
              children: [
                "Couldn't load ",
                this.props.what || "this section",
                ". Check your internet connection, then reload the page.",
              ],
            }),
            e.jsx("button", {
              onClick: () => window.location.reload(),
              className: "px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-semibold text-sm",
              style: { background: "#e0a83b", color: "#0a0818" },
              children: "Reload page",
            }),
          ],
        }),
      });
    }
    return e.jsx(i.Suspense, {
      fallback: this.props.fallback,
      children: this.props.children,
    });
  }
}
const Pv = `Fill out the provided QuizIt! template with questions based on the following topics:

# TOPIC HERE

or the attached pptx or files.

Use the template's exact column headers: Scenario, Type, Question, Option A, Option B, Option C, Option D, Correct, Reference Answer, Starter Code, Time Limit (sec), Points. Do not rename, reorder, or add columns.

Generate:

Identification: 5 items, 3 points each, 180 seconds each
Multiple Choice: 10 items, 2 points each, 180 seconds each
Fill in the Blank: 5 items, 5 points each, 240 seconds each
Short Answer: 5 items, 5 points each, 460 seconds each
Code: 5 items, 10 points each, 480 seconds each — Programming Language: Python
Scenario-based: at least 2 scenarios, each with 2-3 questions attached (any mix of the types above), 3 points each, 240 seconds each — these questions count toward the per-type totals above, not in addition to them

Follow these exact formatting rules per type, since the import is validated strictly and will reject rows that don't match:

Scenario — required, per the Generate list above (at least 2 scenarios, 2-3 attached questions each). A scenario is a shared problem statement, case description, or passage that MULTIPLE questions attach to — shown above each attached question every time it's displayed, and independently, any question type can attach to one (mix Multiple Choice, Identification, Short Answer, etc. under the same scenario freely). Every scenario-attached question uses the 3 points / 240 seconds values from the Generate list above, regardless of its Type — this OVERRIDES that type's own points/seconds line for these questions specifically; only standalone questions of that type use the type's own values. To attach two or more questions to the SAME scenario, repeat that scenario's text EXACTLY, word for word, in the Scenario column on EACH of those rows — the import groups rows into one shared scenario purely by exact text match, so even a single extra space or a reworded sentence creates a second, separate scenario instead of joining the first. Leave Scenario blank on every other row — a standalone question with no shared context is still the default and should remain the majority of the quiz.

Multiple Choice — fill Option A through Option D ; Correct must be a single letter (A, B, C, or D) that matches one of the FILLED options, never a blank one.

Identification — Reference Answer must contain the single correct answer. Leave Option A–D blank.

Fill in the Blank — the Question text must contain one or more blanks written as four or more underscores (e.g. ____). Reference Answer must list exactly one answer per blank, in left-to-right order, separated by a single pipe character with no extra spaces (e.g. photosynthesis|chlorophyll for a question with two blanks). The number of answers in Reference Answer must exactly equal the number of blanks in the Question — mismatches are the single most common cause of import errors, so double-check this count for every row before finalizing.

Short Answer — Reference Answer should contain a concise model answer used for grading reference; keep it to one or two sentences.

Code — Starter Code must contain ONLY the setup/scaffold (imports, function signature, a placeholder like # your code here) and must NOT contain the answer or a completed solution. The complete working solution goes ONLY in Reference Answer, which is used for grading and is never shown to the student. Do not duplicate the solution in both columns, and do not leave Reference Answer empty for code questions.

Before producing the final output, review every Fill in the Blank row specifically and recount blanks vs. Reference Answer entries — this is the error most likely to make the import fail. Separately, review every row where Scenario is filled in and confirm the text is copied EXACTLY the same, character for character, across every row meant to share that scenario — the most common scenario-related error is two rows that were clearly meant to share one scenario ending up with slightly different text and silently becoming two. Finally, confirm the output actually contains at least 2 distinct scenarios with 2-3 questions attached to each, as required above — it's easy to draft a couple of scenario rows early and then default back to standalone questions for the rest of the file without meaning to.

Points — this test case's share of its problem's total score. For each problem, the sum of its test cases' Points here should equal that problem's own Points value on the Problems sheet, so the breakdown adds up cleanly (e.g. a 10-point problem with 4 test cases might use 2, 2, 3, 3).

function Mv(t, a) {
  const d = URL.createObjectURL(
      new Blob([t], { type: "text/plain;charset=utf-8" }),
    ),
    m = document.createElement("a");
  ((m.href = d),
    (m.download = a),
    document.body.appendChild(m),
    m.click(),
    document.body.removeChild(m),
    URL.revokeObjectURL(d));
}

function Ov(t = "W", a) {

      ? crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(6))

}

function $v(t) {

    `scr=${a.screenWidth || 0}x${a.screenHeight || 0}`,

      ? `orient=${String(a.orientation).replace(/-primary|-secondary/, "")}`

    .slice(0, 160);
}

function si(t) {
  if (!t) return "";
  const a = Uv(),
    d = {
      Control: "Ctrl",
      OS: a ? "Cmd" : "Win",
      Meta: a ? "Cmd" : "Win",
      Alt: a ? "Option" : "Alt",
      AltGraph: "AltGr",
    },
    m = [
      t.ctrlKey && "Ctrl",
      t.altKey && d.Alt,
      t.metaKey && d.Meta,
      t.shiftKey && "Shift",
    ].filter(Boolean),
    f = t.key === " " ? "Space" : String(t.key || ""),
    x = ["Control", "Alt", "Meta", "Shift", "OS", "AltGraph"].includes(f),
    b = x
      ? d[f] || f
      : f.length === 1
        ? f.toUpperCase()
        : f === "Backspace"
          ? "Delete"
          : f;
  return (x ? [...m.filter((y) => y !== b), b] : [...m, b])
    .join("+")
    .slice(0, 24);
}
function Bv(t) {

    !!t &&

function Qv(t) {
  if (!t || !(t.ctrlKey || t.metaKey || t.altKey)) return !1;
  const a = String(t.key || "").toLowerCase();
  return a === "backspace" || a === "delete"
    ? !0
    : t.altKey && !t.ctrlKey && !t.metaKey
      ? !1
      : [
          "arrowleft",
          "arrowright",
          "arrowup",
          "arrowdown",
          "home",
          "end",
          "a",
          "z",
          "y",
        ].includes(a);
}

              ? "Recorded (not counted)"

              },

            );
          }),
    ],
  });

const Yh = { background: "#14102e", borderRadius: 12, padding: 14 },
  zc = {
    padding: "8px 14px",
    borderRadius: 10,
    fontSize: 13,
    fontWeight: 600,
    cursor: "pointer",
    border: "none",
  },
  wi = { ...zc, background: "#e0a83b", color: "#0a0818" },
  Kh = { ...zc, background: "#3b3566", color: "#e6e1ff" },
  Vh = { ...zc, background: "#e0506a", color: "#fff" },
  Cn = {
    background: "#0a0818",
    border: "1px solid #3b3566",
    borderRadius: 8,
    padding: "8px 10px",
    color: "#fff",
    fontSize: 14,
  },
  xt = (t, a, d) => We(t, a)(d || {}).then((m) => m.data),
  An = (t) =>
    t && t.message && !/^(internal|functions\/)/i.test(t.message)
      ? t.message
      : "Something went wrong. Please try again.",
  s1 = [
    "January",
    "February",
    "March",
    "April",
    "May",
    "June",
    "July",
    "August",
    "September",
    "October",
    "November",
    "December",
  ],
  Nc = (t) =>
    t
      ? new Date(t).toLocaleDateString(void 0, {
          year: "numeric",
          month: "long",
          day: "numeric",
          timeZone: "Asia/Manila",
        })
      : "",
  n1 = (t) => {
    const [a, d] = String(t || "01-01")
      .split("-")
      .map(Number);
    return { m: a || 1, d: d || 1 };
  },
  Gx = (t, a) => String(t).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(a).padStart(2, "0");
function ni({ label: t, value: a, onChange: d }) {
  const { m, d: f } = n1(a);
  return e.jsxs("div", {
    className: "flex items-center gap-2 mb-2",
    children: [
      e.jsx("div", { className: "text-sm w-28 opacity-80", children: t }),
      e.jsx("select", {
        style: Cn,
        value: m,
        onChange: (x) => d(Gx(Number(x.target.value), f)),
        children: s1.map((x, b) =>
          e.jsx("option", { value: b + 1, children: x }, x),
        ),
      }),
      e.jsx("input", {
        style: { ...Cn, width: 64 },
        type: "number",
        min: 1,
        max: 31,
        value: f,
        onChange: (x) =>
          d(Gx(m, Math.min(31, Math.max(1, Number(x.target.value) || 1)))),
      }),
    ],
  });
}

function ac({ functions: t, value: a, onChange: d }) {
  const [m, f] = i.useState({
    loading: !0,
    linked: !1,
    classes: [],
    error: "",
  });
  i.useEffect(() => {
    let b = !0;
    return (
      (async () => {
        try {
          if (!(await xt(t, "getTalaanStatus")).linked) {
            b && f({ loading: !1, linked: !1, classes: [], error: "" });
            return;
          }
          const y = await xt(t, "listTalaanClasses");
          b &&
            f({ loading: !1, linked: !0, classes: y.classes || [], error: "" });
        } catch (N) {
          b && f({ loading: !1, linked: !1, classes: [], error: An(N) });
        }
      })(),
      () => {
        b = !1;
      }
    );
  }, [t]);
  const x = (b) =>
    e.jsxs("div", {
      className: "p-3 rounded-xl mb-4",
      style: { background: "#14102e" },
      children: [
        e.jsx("div", {
          className: "text-sm font-semibold",
          children: "TALA-AN class list (optional)",
        }),
        b,
      ],
    });
  return m.loading
    ? x(
        e.jsx("div", {
          className: "text-xs opacity-50 mt-1",
          children: "Checking your TALA-AN link…",
        }),
      )
    : m.error
      ? x(
          e.jsx("div", {
            className: "text-xs mt-1",
            style: { color: "#e0506a" },
            children: m.error,
          }),
        )
      : m.linked
        ? x(
            e.jsxs("div", {
              children: [
                e.jsx("div", {
                  className: "text-xs opacity-50 mb-2",
                  children:
                    "Only students on this class can answer. Turns on “Require TALA-AN login” for this session. QuizIt copies the Student ID numbers only.",
                }),
                m.classes.length === 0
                  ? e.jsx("div", {
                      className: "text-xs opacity-60",
                      children:
                        "No active classes found in your TALA-AN account.",
                    })
                  : e.jsxs("select", {
                      style: { ...Cn, width: "100%" },
                      value: a,
                      onChange: (b) => d(b.target.value),
                      children: [
                        e.jsx("option", {
                          value: "",
                          children: "No class list — anyone with the code",
                        }),
                        m.classes.map((b) =>
                          e.jsxs(
                            "option",
                            {
                              value: b.classId,
                              children: [
                                `${b.subjectCode} ${b.subjectName}`.trim(),
                                " ",
                                b.schoolYear ? `· ${b.schoolYear}` : "",
                                " ",
                                b.semester ? `· ${b.semester}` : "",
                              ],
                            },
                            b.classId,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    }),
              ],
            }),
          )
        : x(
            e.jsx("div", {
              className: "text-xs opacity-60 mt-1",
              children:
                "Not linked yet. To limit a session to a TALA-AN class, link your account first: Settings (top of the dashboard) → TALA-AN class lists.",
            }),
          );
}
function oc({ functions: t, code: a, ended: d, kind: m = "quiz" }) {

    [y, R] = i.useState(null),
    [S, I] = i.useState(null),
    [E, O] = i.useState(""),
    [Z, Y] = i.useState(""),
    [F, te] = i.useState(!1),
    [P, K] = i.useState(null),
    Ie = i.useCallback(async () => {
      try {
        const [pe, Ht] = await Promise.all([
          xt(t, "getSessionRoster", { code: a, kind: m }),
          xt(t, "getTalaanStatus"),
        ]);
        (x(pe), R(!!Ht.linked));
      } catch (pe) {
        x({ error: An(pe) });
      }
    }, [t, a, m]);
  i.useEffect(() => {
    (x(null), N(!1), K(null), Ie());
  }, [Ie]);

      (te(!0), K(null));
      try {
        (await pe(), Ht && K({ ok: !0, text: Ht }), await Ie());
      } catch (Gs) {
        K({ ok: !1, text: An(Gs) });
      }
      te(!1);
    },
    tt = async () => {
      try {
        I((await xt(t, "listTalaanClasses")).classes || []);
      } catch (pe) {
        K({ ok: !1, text: An(pe) });
      }
    };
  if (!f || f.error) return null;
  const ie = f.tagged && !f.removed && f.count > 0;
  return !ie && !f.removed && y === !1
    ? null
    : e.jsxs("div", {

                    className: "font-semibold",

                  ie
                    ? e.jsxs("span", {
                        children: [

                            ? ` · ${f.overrides.length} allowed late`
                            : "",
                        ],
                      })
                    : f.removed
                      ? e.jsx("span", {
                          style: { color: "#e0a83b" },
                          children:
                            "removed on the removal date — new students are told to ask you",
                        })
                      : e.jsx("span", {
                          className: "opacity-60",
                          children: "none (anyone with the code can join)",
                        }),
                ],
              }),
              e.jsx("button", {
                style: Kh,
                onClick: () => {
                  (N((pe) => !pe), !S && y && tt());
                },
                children: b ? "Hide" : "Manage",
              }),
            ],
          }),
          ie &&
            f.daysLeft != null &&
            f.daysLeft <= 7 &&
            e.jsxs("div", {
              className: "text-xs mt-2",
              style: { color: "#e0a83b" },

function Hx(t) {
  if (!t || t.nodeType !== 1) return !1;
  const a = t.tagName;
  if (a === "TEXTAREA" || a === "SELECT") return !0;
  if (a === "INPUT") {
    const d = (t.type || "text").toLowerCase();
    return ![
      "button",
      "checkbox",
      "radio",
      "submit",
      "reset",
      "range",
      "color",
      "file",
      "image",
    ].includes(d);
  }
  return !!t.isContentEditable;
}

function Jh(t, a) {
  if (!t || t.repeat) return null;
  const d = t.key;
  if (d === "Alt" || d === "Meta" || d === "OS" || d === "AltGraph")
    return d === "Alt" ? "the Alt key" : "the Windows/Command key";
  if (d === "Control") return a ? null : "the Ctrl key";
  if (t.metaKey || t.altKey)
    return t.metaKey ? "the Windows/Command key" : "the Alt key";
  if (t.ctrlKey) {
    if (!a) return "the Ctrl key";
    if (
      d === "Tab" ||
      d === "PageUp" ||
      d === "PageDown" ||
      /^[1-9]$/.test(d) ||
      /^[tnwTNW]$/.test(d)
    )
      return "a Ctrl tab/window shortcut";
  }
  return null;
}

      );
    }, [t, a]),
    e.jsx(Q, {
      children: e.jsxs("div", {
        className:
          "flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 text-center w-full max-w-[max(461px,55vw)]",
        children: [
          e.jsx("div", {
            style: { fontSize: 40, marginBottom: 10 },
            children: "🔒",
          }),
          e.jsx("div", {
            className: "sora text-xl font-bold mb-2",
            style: { color: "#e74c6e" },
            children: "You are not allowed to continue",
          }),
          e.jsx("div", {
            className: "opacity-60 mb-8",
            children:
              "You were blocked from your previous attempt, so you can't continue with this quiz. Please ask your teacher if you think this is a mistake.",
          }),
          e.jsx("button", {
            onClick: m,
            className: "w-full py-3 rounded-xl font-semibold",
            style: { background: "#3b8ee0", color: "#0a0818" },
            children: "Back to main screen",
          }),
        ],
      }),
    })
  );
}

    b = ({ stacked: N }) =>
      e.jsxs("div", {

        children: [
          e.jsx("button", {

            },

            style: {

              color: "#f2eefc",
              border: "1px solid #3b3566",
            },
            children: "Subscription and Pricing",
          }),
          e.jsx("button", {
            onClick: () => {
              (m(), x(!1));
            },
            className: `px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold ${N ? "w-full text-left" : ""}`,
            style: { background: "#1e1a3d", color: "#f2eefc" },
            children: "Login",
          }),
          e.jsx("button", {
            onClick: () => {
              (d(), x(!1));
            },

              boxShadow: "0 3px 0 #a5780f",
            },

          }),
        ],
      });
  return e.jsxs("div", {
    className: "sticky top-0 z-40 backdrop-blur",

      background: "rgba(10,8,24,0.9)",

        e.jsx("div", {
          className: "md:hidden px-6 pb-4",

        }),
    ],
  });
}

          children: d,
        }),
    ],
  });
}

          className: "opacity-70 text-sm md:text-base",

    ],
  });

  async function $y(
    s,
    {
      questionText: o,
      maxPoints: r,
      referenceAnswer: c,
      studentAnswer: l,
      questionType: h,
      scenarioText: n,
      customSystemPrompt: u,
    },
  ) {
    var ve, Ne, Ae;
    const g =
        {
          code: "Code / programming",
          identification:
            "Identification (one specific correct answer expected)",

            "Fill in the Blank (one or more blanks, each with an expected answer — reference lists them in order)",
        }[h] || "Short answer",
      v = (u || w1).replace(/\{maxPoints\}/g, String(r)),
      j =
        (n
          ? `Scenario / shared context for this question:
${n}

`
          : "") +
        `Question type: ${g}
Question: ${o}
Max points: ${r}
` +
        (c
          ? `Reference answer / expected solution: ${c}
`
          : "") +
        `Student's answer:
${l || "(blank — no answer submitted)"}`,
      A = await fetch("https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          Authorization: `Bearer ${s}`,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model: "gpt-4o-mini",
          messages: [
            { role: "system", content: v },
            { role: "user", content: j },
          ],
          response_format: { type: "json_object" },
          temperature: 0,
        }),
      });
    if (!A.ok) {
      const H = await A.text().catch(() => "");
      throw new Error(`OpenAI API error ${A.status}: ${H.slice(0, 200)}`);
    }
    const C = await A.json(),
      L =
        (Ae =
          (Ne =
            (ve = C == null ? void 0 : C.choices) == null ? void 0 : ve[0]) ==
          null
            ? void 0
            : Ne.message) == null
          ? void 0
          : Ae.content;
    if (!L) throw new Error("No content in OpenAI response");
    let _;
    try {
      _ = JSON.parse(L);
    } catch {
      throw new Error("Couldn't parse AI response as JSON");
    }
    let W = Number(_.score);
    (Number.isNaN(W) && (W = 0), (W = Math.max(0, Math.min(r, W))));
    const T = typeof _.reasoning == "string" ? _.reasoning.slice(0, 500) : "";
    return { score: W, reasoning: T };
  }
  async function Uy(
    s,

${c}

${u}

${r || "(blank — no answer submitted)"}`,
